{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MGWTgKoQpEwCVujCbmNFDQ","type":"tool_result","content":"# recs_eval release notes\n\n## 1.4.2 (2026-08-20)\n- `ope` writes the four files in `docs/outputs/ope_outputs.md` so the launch review has one artefact.\n\n## 1.4.0 (2026-07-02)\n- Offline gate: replay a candidate ranker on logged responses and report clicks per matched impression, scaled to a\n  five-slot row (`CTR@5`).\n- Gate runs on production-stream responses, where the candidate lists are logged.\n\n## 1.3.0 (2026-05-11)\n- Candidate list logging (`rec_candidates`) sampled for production traffic; all exploration requests are logged.\n# Recs / Experimentation sync, 2 September 2026\n\n**Ranking (v7 owner).** The gate had v7 well ahead of v6 before we ran AB-1182, and the gate has been our launch\ncriterion all year. The A/B came back negative with a sample-ratio warning, so the experiment is the thing to\ndistrust, not the model. We would like to ship v7.\n\n**Experimentation.** The arm split is by member and the member counts are balanced (the readout has the table). The\nratio warning is on responses per arm, and responses are not what we assign. We are not aware of a bucketing defect.\n\n**Ranking (v7_pd owner).** v7_pd trains on the exploration stream instead of the production logs. It has never been in\nan A/B. The gate likes it even more than v7.\n\n**Analytics.** Two things bother me. The gate scored v7 at a large positive number and the A/B measured a negative\none, so at least one of them is wrong about v7. And whatever is wrong there also applies to the v7_pd number, which\nnobody has checked against anything.\n\n**Action.** Personalisation Analytics to produce a defensible number per ranker and a launch recommendation.\n# Ranker launch policy\n\nOwner: Personalisation leadership.\n\n1. A candidate ranker may be launched only if its estimated improvement over the production ranker on\n   **home-row clicks per slate decision** (`docs/metrics/home_row.md`) has a two-sided 95% confidence interval whose lower bound is above zero.\n2. If more than one candidate qualifies, launch the one with the higher estimate.\n3. If none qualifies, keep the production ranker.\n\nThe estimate must cover the whole evaluation window and all devices.\n# Home-row logging schema (`data/logs.sqlite`)\n\nTimes are UTC, `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS`.\n\n**Extract composition.** The evaluation extract contains every session in the exploration stream and a random sample\nof production sessions, so exploration is heavily over-represented relative to live traffic\n(`serving/config/serving.yaml`). Stream membership is decided per session, before A/B assignment.\n\n## `rec_serves` — one row per served response\n\n| Column | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `serve_id` | id of the response |\n| `session_id`, `user_id` | client session; signed-in member |\n| `device` | `web`, `mobile`, `tv` |\n| `surface` | placement (`home`) |\n| `served_at` | when the response was returned |\n| `stream` | serving stream: `prod_rank` or `explore_shuffle` |\n| `ab_arm` | experiment arm for AB-1182 (`v7`, `control`, `none`) |\n| `slot_1` … `slot_5` | the titles in the response, in order |\n| `propensity` | probability with which the ranker produced this response |\n| `candidate_count` | number of titles retrieved for the request |\n\n## `rec_candidates` — the titles the ranker scored\n\nWritten by the ranker for a sampled share of requests (all exploration requests, a small share of production).\n\n| Column | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `serve_id` | response the candidate list belongs to |\n| `item_id` | retrieved title |\n| `score_v6`, `score_v7`, `score_v7_pd` | model scores for the title on this request |\n| `filter_reason` | reason the rules layer suppressed the title (`already_watched`, `licence_window`), NULL if it was not suppressed |\n\n## `click_events` — one row per click\n\n| Column | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `click_id` | id of the click |\n| `serve_id` | response the click was attributed to |\n| `click_time` | when the click happened |\n| `position` | slot clicked (1–5) |\n| `item_id` | title clicked |\n\n## `items`\n\n`item_id`, `title`, `genre`, `release_year`.\n# Evaluation outputs: specification\n\nOwner: Personalisation Analytics. `python -m recs_eval ope --logs <db> --out <dir>` writes four files. Numbers are\nwritten unrounded.\n\nDefinitions used below come from `docs/metrics/home_row.md`.\n\n## `decisions.csv`\n\nOne row per slate decision and slot, for every decision in the extract (all streams, all devices).\n\n| Column | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `decision_id` | the `serve_id` of the response that created the decision |\n| `stream` | serving stream of the decision |\n| `device` | device of the decision |\n| `decided_at` | `served_at` of the response that created the decision |\n| `position` | slot, 1–5 |\n| `item_id` | the title the decision placed in that slot |\n| `clicked` | 1 if the member clicked that slot of this decision, else 0 |\n\n## `target_slates.csv`\n\nThe row each candidate ranker would have served, for every decision whose candidate list is in `rec_candidates`.\nOne row per decision, ranker and slot: `decision_id`, `policy` (`v6`, `v7`, `v7_pd`), `position`, `item_id`.\n\n## `policy_values.csv`\n\nOne row per ranker (`v6`, `v7`, `v7_pd`):\n\n| Column | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `value` | estimated home-row clicks per slate decision over the extract's decisions if that ranker had served them |\n| `ci_low`, `ci_high` | 95% interval for `value` |\n| `lift_vs_v6` | estimated difference in clicks per decision against the production ranker (0 for `v6`) |\n| `lift_ci_low`, `lift_ci_high` | 95% interval for the lift |\n\n## `launch.json`\n\n`{\"launch\": \"v6\" | \"v7\" | \"v7_pd\"}` applying `docs/launch_policy.md` (`v6` means keep the production ranker).\n\nModel scores are unique within a request, so a ranker's row is determined by its scores.\n# Home-row engagement metric\n\nOwner: Personalisation Analytics.\n\n- **Metric: home-row clicks per slate decision.** The number of the row's five slots that the member clicked,\n  averaged over slate decisions. Slots the member never looked at count as zero, not as missing.\n- **Slate decision:** one ranking choice for a member. The service makes the choice once and then keeps returning it\n  for that member's session on that surface until the stored response expires; a later request that gets a fresh\n  ranking is a new decision.\n- **Serve:** one response returned to a client. A decision can be served more than once.\n- **Click:** a click on a title in the row. A member can click more than one slot of a decision; a slot counts once.\n\nThis metric is the one the launch policy refers to. Click-through rate per response and click-through rate per\nrendered impression are reporting conveniences and are not the launch metric: both depend on how often clients\nre-request the row, which itself depends on what was served.","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"230ed98a-7798-4532-801d-718b6246ddc1","uuid":"5481f43f-f23b-472a-a927-0fe5d7b8c412","timestamp":"2026-10-01T05:56:34.140Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"# recs_eval release notes\n\n## 1.4.2 (2026-08-20)\n- `ope` writes the four files in `docs/outputs/ope_outputs.md` so the launch review has one artefact.\n\n## 1.4.0 (2026-07-02)\n- Offline gate: replay a candidate ranker on logged responses and report clicks per matched impression, scaled to a\n  five-slot row (`CTR@5`).\n- Gate runs on production-stream responses, where the candidate lists are logged.\n\n## 1.3.0 (2026-05-11)\n- Candidate list logging (`rec_candidates`) sampled for production traffic; all exploration requests are logged.\n# Recs / Experimentation sync, 2 September 2026\n\n**Ranking (v7 owner).** The gate had v7 well ahead of v6 before we ran AB-1182, and the gate has been our launch\ncriterion all year. \"SELECT serve_id, item_id, score_v6, score_v7, score_v7_pd, filter_reason FROM rec_candidates\", con)\n\"\"\"Offline gate metrics.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nSLOTS = 5\n\n\ndef ctr_at_5(matched: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Clicks per matched impression, scaled to a five-slot row.\"\"\"\n    g = matched.groupby(\"policy\")[\"clicked\"]\n    out = pd.DataFrame({\"impressions\": g.size(), \"clicks\": g.sum()})\n    out[\"ctr\"] = out[\"clicks\"] / out[\"impressions\"]\n    out[\"value\"] = SLOTS * out[\"ctr\"]\n    out[\"se\"] = SLOTS * np.sqrt(out[\"ctr\"] * (1 - out[\"ctr\"]) / out[\"impressions\"])\n    return out.reset_index()\n\n\ndef with_intervals(values: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    v = values.set_index(\"policy\")\n    base = v.loc[\"v6\"]\n    rows = []\n    for policy, r in v.iterrows():\n        lift = r[\"value\"] - base[\"value\"]\n        lift_se = float(np.sqrt(r[\"se\"] ** 2 + base[\"se\"] ** 2))\n        rows.append({\"policy\": policy, \"value\": r[\"value\"],\n                     \"ci_low\": r[\"value\"] - 1.96 * r[\"se\"], \"ci_high\": r[\"value\"] + 1.96 * r[\"se\"],\n                     \"lift_vs_v6\": lift, \"lift_ci_low\": lift - 1.96 * lift_se,\n                     \"lift_ci_high\": lift + 1.96 * lift_se})\n    return Slots where the candidate ranker agrees with the log are scored with the logged outcome.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pandas as pd\n\nfrom recs_eval import logs\n\nSLOTS = (cand.sort_values([\"serve_id\", col], ascending=[True, False])\n                   .groupby(\"serve_id\").head(SLOTS).copy())\n        top[\"position\"] = top.groupby(\"serve_id\").cumcount() + 1\n        top[\"policy\"] = policy\n        out.append(top[[\"serve_id\", \"policy\", \"position\", \"item_id\"]])\n    return pd.concat(out, ignore_index=True)\n\n\ndef matched(imp: pd.DataFrame, targets: A candidate ranker's row is therefore its top five unsuppressed titles by its own score.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pandas as pd\n\nSLOTS = 5\nMODEL_SCORES = {\"v6\": \"score_v6\", \"v7\": \"score_v7\", \"v7_pd\": \"score_v7_pd\"}\n\n\ndef target_slates(cand: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Top five titles per response for each ranker, after the rules layer's suppressions.\"\"\"\n    cand = cand[cand[\"filter_reason\"].isna()]\n    out = []\n    for policy, col in MODEL_SCORES.items():\n        top = (cand.sort_values([\"serve_id\", col, \"item_id\"], ascending=[True, False, True])\n                   .groupby(\"serve_id\").head(SLOTS).copy())\n        top[\"position\"] = top.groupby(\"serve_id\").cumcount() + 1\n        top[\"policy\"] = policy\n        out.append(top[[\"serve_id\", \"policy\", \"position\", \"item_id\"]])\n    return pd.concat(out, ignore_index=True)\n\n\ndef eligible_counts(cand: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:\n    \"\"\"Number of titles left after the rules layer, per response.\"\"\"\n    return cand[cand[\"filter_reason\"].isna()].groupby(\"serve_id\").size().rename(\"eligible\")\nEOF\ncat > recs_eval/metrics.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Off-policy estimate of home-row clicks per slate decision.\n\nLogging policy: the exploration stream orders the retrieved pool uniformly at random; the rules layer then removes\nsuppressed titles and the first five remaining titles are served. Given the `m` unsuppressed titles, every title is\ntherefore in any given slot with probability 1/m. (The logged `propensity` is the probability of the ranker's\nordering *before* the rules layer, and of the whole ordered slate, so it is not the right weight here.)\n\nEstimator: slot-matched inverse propensity scoring. For a decision and a candidate ranker,\n\n    y = m * sum_k 1[logged title in slot k == ranker's title in slot k] * clicked_k\n\nis unbiased for the ranker's clicks on that decision when a slot's click depends on the member, the title and the\nslot. Only exploration decisions are used (production decisions are deterministic and give no support to other\nrankers). Exploration membership is decided per session at random, so exploration decisions are a sample of all\ndecisions; they are post-stratified by device to the device mix of all decisions in the extract. Intervals are\nnormal, with standard errors clustered by member.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom scipy.stats import norm\n\nEXPLORE_STREAM = \"explore_shuffle\"\nBASELINE = \"v6\"\nZ = float(norm.ppf(0.975))\n\n\ndef per_decision_estimates(decisions: pd.DataFrame, targets: pd.DataFrame, eligible: pd.Series,\n                           policies: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"One row per exploration decision with an IPS click estimate per ranker.\"\"\"\n    exp = decisions[decisions[\"stream\"] == EXPLORE_STREAM]\n    meta = exp.drop_duplicates(\"decision_id\").set_index(\"decision_id\")[[\"user_id\", \"device\"]]\n    meta = meta.join(eligible.rename(\"eligible\"), how=\"inner\")\n    meta = meta[meta[\"eligible\"] > 0]\n    logged = exp[exp[\"decision_id\"].isin(meta.index)][[\"decision_id\", \"position\", \"item_id\", \"clicked\"]]\n    out = meta.copy()\n    for policy in policies:\n        t = targets[targets[\"policy\"] == policy][[\"decision_id\", \"position\", \"item_id\"]]\n        x = logged.merge(t, on=[\"decision_id\", \"position\"], how=\"left\", suffixes=(\"\", \"_target\"))\n        x[\"r\"] = (x[\"item_id\"] == x[\"item_id_target\"]).astype(float) * x[\"clicked\"]\n        hits = x.groupby(\"decision_id\")[\"r\"].sum().reindex(out.index, fill_value=0.0)\n        out[policy] = out[\"eligible\"].astype(float) * hits\n    return out.sort_index()\n\n\ndef _stratum_weights(est: pd.DataFrame, decisions: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:\n    \"\"\"Per-decision weights summing to 1 that give each device its share of all decisions in the extract.\"\"\"\n    share = decisions.drop_duplicates(\"decision_id\")[\"device\"].value_counts(normalize=True)\n    covered = share.index.intersection(est[\"device\"].unique())\n    missing = share.index.difference(covered)\n    if len(missing):\n        print(f\"warning: no exploration decisions for device(s) {sorted(missing)}; \"\n              f\"estimate covers {share.loc[covered].sum():.1%} of decisions\")\n    share = share.loc[covered] / share.loc[covered].sum()\n    n = est[\"device\"].value_counts()\n    return est[\"device\"].map(share / n)\n\n\ndef _estimate(y: pd.Series, w: pd.Series, device: pd.Series, cluster: pd.Series) -> tuple[float, float]:\n    \"\"\"Post-stratified mean and its member-clustered linearised standard error.\"\"\"\n    mean_by_device = (w * y).groupby(device).sum() / w.groupby(device).sum()\n    value = float((w * y).sum())\n    infl = w * (y - device.map(mean_by_device))\n    sums = infl.groupby(cluster).sum()\n    g = len(sums)\n    se = float(np.sqrt(g / (g - 1) * (sums ** 2).sum())) if g > 1 else float(\"nan\")\n    return value, se\n\n\ndef policy_values(est: pd.DataFrame, decisions: pd.DataFrame, policies: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    w = _stratum_weights(est, decisions)\n    rows = []\n    for policy in policies:\n        value, se = _estimate(est[policy], w, est[\"device\"], est[\"user_id\"])\n        lift, lift_se = _estimate(est[policy] - est[BASELINE], w, est[\"device\"], est[\"user_id\"])\n        if policy == BASELINE:\n            lift, lift_se = 0.0, 0.0\n        rows.append({\"policy\": policy, \"value\": value,\n                     \"ci_low\": value - Z * se, \"ci_high\": value + Z * se,\n                     \"lift_vs_v6\": lift, \"lift_ci_low\": lift - Z * lift_se, \"lift_ci_high\": lift + Z * lift_se})\n    return pd.DataFrame(rows)\nEOF\ncat > recs_eval/cli.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"`python -m recs_eval ope --logs data/logs.sqlite --out out/ope`\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom recs_eval import logs, metrics, replay, report\n\n\ndef ope(db: Path, out: Path, ttl_seconds: int | None = None) -> None:\n    ttl = logs.cache_ttl_seconds() if ttl_seconds is None else ttl_seconds\n    con = logs.connect(db)\n    try:\n        dec = logs.decisions(con, ttl)\n        cand = logs.candidates(con)\n    finally:\n        con.close()\n\n    # candidate lists are written when the ranker runs, i.e. by the serve that created a decision\n    cand = cand[cand[\"serve_id\"].isin(dec[\"decision_id\"])]\n    targets = replay.target_slates(cand).rename(columns={\"serve_id\": \"decision_id\"})\n    eligible = replay.eligible_counts(cand)\n    est = metrics.per_decision_estimates(dec, targets, eligible, report.ORDER)\n    values = metrics.policy_values(est, dec, report.ORDER)\n\n    decisions = dec[[\"decision_id\", \"stream\", \"device\", \"decided_at\", \"position\", \"item_id\", \"clicked\"]]\n    targets = targets.sort_values([\"decision_id\", \"policy\", \"position\"],\n                                  key=lambda c: c.map(report.ORDER.index) if c.name == \"policy\" else c)\n    report.write_all(out, decisions, targets.reset_index(drop=True), values)\n\n\ndef main(argv=None) -> None:\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"recs_eval\")\n    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    r = sub.add_parser(\"ope\", help=\"evaluate the candidate rankers and write the launch recommendation\")\n    r.add_argument(\"--logs\", default=\"data/logs.sqlite\")\n    r.add_argument(\"--out\", default=\"out/ope\")\n    r.add_argument(\"--cache-ttl\", type=int, default=None,\n                   help=\"response cache TTL in seconds (default: serving/config/serving.yaml)\")\n    args = ap.parse_args(argv)\n    ope(Path(args.logs), Path(args.out), args.cache_ttl)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nEOF\npython - <<'EOF'\nimport re\np='recs_eval/report.py'; t=open(p).read()\nt=t.replace('''        launch = max(qualifies, key=lambda p: A candidate ranker's row is therefore its top five unsuppressed titles by its own score.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pandas as pd\n\nSLOTS = 5\nMODEL_SCORES = {\"v6\": \"score_v6\", \"v7\": \"score_v7\", \"v7_pd\": \"score_v7_pd\"}\n\n\ndef target_slates(cand: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Top five titles per response for each ranker, after the rules layer's suppressions.\"\"\"\n    cand = cand[cand[\"filter_reason\"].isna()]\n    out = []\n    for policy, col in MODEL_SCORES.items():\n        top = (cand.sort_values([\"serve_id\", col, \"item_id\"], ascending=[True, False, True])\n                   .groupby(\"serve_id\").head(SLOTS).copy())\n        top[\"position\"] = top.groupby(\"serve_id\").cumcount() + 1\n        top[\"policy\"] = policy\n        out.append(top[[\"serve_id\", \"policy\", \"position\", \"item_id\"]])\n    return pd.concat(out, ignore_index=True)\n\n\ndef eligible_counts(cand: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:\n    \"\"\"Number of titles left after the rules layer, per response.\"\"\"\n    return cand[cand[\"filter_reason\"].isna()].groupby(\"serve_id\").size().rename(\"eligible\")\nEOF\ncat > recs_eval/metrics.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Off-policy estimate of home-row clicks per slate decision.\n\nLogging policy: the exploration stream orders the retrieved pool uniformly at random; the rules layer then removes\nsuppressed titles and the first five remaining titles are served. Given the `m` unsuppressed titles, every title is\ntherefore in any given slot with probability 1/m. (The logged `propensity` is the probability of the ranker's\nordering *before* the rules layer, and of the whole ordered slate, so it is not the right weight here.)\n\nEstimator: slot-matched inverse propensity scoring. For a decision and a candidate ranker,\n\n    y = m * sum_k 1[logged title in slot k == ranker's title in slot k] * clicked_k\n\nis unbiased for the ranker's clicks on that decision when a slot's click depends on the member, the title and the\nslot. Only exploration decisions are used (production decisions are deterministic and give no support to other\nrankers). Exploration membership is decided per session at random, so exploration decisions are a sample of all\ndecisions; they are post-stratified by device to the device mix of all decisions in the extract. Intervals are\nnormal, with standard errors clustered by member.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom scipy.stats import norm\n\nEXPLORE_STREAM = \"explore_shuffle\"\nBASELINE = \"v6\"\nZ = float(norm.ppf(0.975))\n\n\ndef per_decision_estimates(decisions: pd.DataFrame, targets: pd.DataFrame, eligible: pd.Series,\n                           policies: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"One row per exploration decision with an IPS click estimate per ranker.\"\"\"\n    exp = decisions[decisions[\"stream\"] == EXPLORE_STREAM]\n    meta = exp.drop_duplicates(\"decision_id\").set_index(\"decision_id\")[[\"user_id\", \"device\"]]\n    meta = meta.join(eligible.rename(\"eligible\"), how=\"inner\")\n    meta = meta[meta[\"eligible\"] > 0]\n    logged = exp[exp[\"decision_id\"].isin(meta.index)][[\"decision_id\", \"position\", \"item_id\", \"clicked\"]]\n    out = meta.copy()\n    for policy in policies:\n        t = targets[targets[\"policy\"] == policy][[\"decision_id\", \"position\", \"item_id\"]]\n        x = logged.merge(t, on=[\"decision_id\", \"position\"], how=\"left\", suffixes=(\"\", \"_target\"))\n        x[\"r\"] = (x[\"item_id\"] == x[\"item_id_target\"]).astype(float) * x[\"clicked\"]\n        hits = x.groupby(\"decision_id\")[\"r\"].sum().reindex(out.index, fill_value=0.0)\n        out[policy] = out[\"eligible\"].astype(float) * hits\n    return out.sort_index()\n\n\ndef _stratum_weights(est: pd.DataFrame, decisions: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.Series:\n    \"\"\"Per-decision weights summing to 1 that give each device its share of all decisions in the extract.\"\"\"\n    share = decisions.drop_duplicates(\"decision_id\")[\"device\"].value_counts(normalize=True)\n    covered = share.index.intersection(est[\"device\"].unique())\n    missing = share.index.difference(covered)\n    if len(missing):\n        print(f\"warning: no exploration decisions for device(s) {sorted(missing)}; \"\n              f\"estimate covers {share.loc[covered].sum():.1%} of decisions\")\n    share = share.loc[covered] / share.loc[covered].sum()\n    n = est[\"device\"].value_counts()\n    return est[\"device\"].map(share / n)\n\n\ndef _estimate(y: pd.Series, w: pd.Series, device: pd.Series, cluster: pd.Series) -> tuple[float, float]:\n    \"\"\"Post-stratified mean and its member-clustered linearised standard error.\"\"\"\n    mean_by_device = (w * y).groupby(device).sum() / w.groupby(device).sum()\n    value = float((w * y).sum())\n    infl = w * (y - device.map(mean_by_device))\n    sums = infl.groupby(cluster).sum()\n    g = len(sums)\n    se = float(np.sqrt(g / (g - 1) * (sums ** 2).sum())) if g > 1 else float(\"nan\")\n    return value, se\n\n\ndef policy_values(est: pd.DataFrame, decisions: pd.DataFrame, policies: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    w = _stratum_weights(est, decisions)\n    rows = []\n    for policy in policies:\n        value, se = _estimate(est[policy], w, est[\"device\"], est[\"user_id\"])\n        lift, lift_se = _estimate(est[policy] - est[BASELINE], w, est[\"device\"], est[\"user_id\"])\n        if policy == BASELINE:\n            lift, lift_se = 0.0, 0.0\n        rows.append({\"policy\": policy, \"value\": value,\n                     \"ci_low\": value - Z * se, \"ci_high\": value + Z * se,\n                     \"lift_vs_v6\": lift, \"lift_ci_low\": lift - Z * lift_se, \"lift_ci_high\": lift + Z * lift_se})\n    return pd.DataFrame(rows)\nEOF\ncat > recs_eval/cli.py metrics, replay, report\n\n\ndef ope(db: Path, out: Path, ttl_seconds: int | None = None) -> None:\n    ttl = logs.cache_ttl_seconds() if ttl_seconds is None else ttl_seconds\n    con = logs.connect(db)\n    try:\n        dec = logs.decisions(con, ttl)\n        cand = logs.candidates(con)\n    finally:\n        con.close()\n\n    # candidate lists are written when the ranker runs, eligible = replay.eligible_counts(cand)\n    est = metrics.per_decision_estimates(dec, targets, eligible, report.ORDER)\n    values = metrics.policy_values(est, dec, report.ORDER)\n\n    decisions = {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfb1SagFEMPuMbo5nZHTS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've rebuilt the estimate and regenerated the outputs in `out/ope/`. **The recommendation is to launch v7_pd.** It is the only candidate whose lift clears the launch bar. v7 is reliably worse than v6, which is what AB-1182 found.\n\n| Ranker | Clicks per slate decision (95% CI) | Lift vs v6 (95% CI) |\n|---|---|---|\n| v6 (production) | 0.390 (0.371 – 0.410) | — |\n| v7 | 0.361 (0.343 – 0.379) | **−0.029** (−0.053 – −0.006), about −7% |\n| v7_pd | 0.469 (0.448 – 0.490) | **+0.079** (+0.053 – +0.104), about +20% |\n\n### What was wrong with the old number\nThe old offline gate wasn't measuring this metric, and it was biased toward a ranker that looked like production.\n\n1. **It scored the wrong data.** It only used the production stream, where v6 decides every row. A candidate earned credit only on titles v6 had already chosen to show, in whatever slot v6 put them. That rewards resembling v6 and says little about what the candidate would have served on its own. There was no correction for this.\n2. **It used the wrong unit.** It counted clicks per matched impression, multiplied by 5, not clicks per slate decision. The service caches a row for 10 minutes and re-serves it, so about 36,000 of the 166,000 logged serves are repeats of an existing decision. CTR per serve is the measure `docs/metrics/home_row.md` specifically rules out.\n3. **The propensity in the logs is the wrong weight.** It is recorded before the rules layer removes already-watched and out-of-licence titles, and it is for the whole ordered row. That is why the June notebook's weights blew up to about 10⁷ and had to be clipped arbitrarily.\n\n### What it does now\n- **Groups serves into decisions** using the cache rule from `serving.yaml`: same session, surface and row, within 600 s. A slot clicked on any serve of a decision counts once.\n- **Values each ranker on the exploration stream**, where the row order is random. Each slot is weighted by how many titles were left after the rules layer.\n- **Covers the whole window and all devices**, weighting by each device's share of decisions in the extract. Intervals allow for repeat decisions by the same member.\n\nThere's nothing specific to this extract. The cache lifetime is read from the serving config, and the launch rule is applied as written. Two runs gave byte-identical outputs, and a run takes about 20 seconds.\n\n### Checks against reality\n- **v6 matches what production actually got.** The estimate (0.390) agrees with the clicks per decision actually logged on the production stream (0.385).\n- **The rows are reconstructed correctly.** The replayed v6 and v7 rows match the rows production actually served exactly, in every case.\n- **v7 agrees with the A/B in sign.** AB-1182 per decision was 0.388 for v6 against 0.375 for v7, a drop of 0.013. Over the same weeks, this estimate puts v7 0.039 below v6. That is within noise, and v7 is negative in every month. The A/B was not broken; the gate was.\n- **v7_pd's lift is stable.** It is +0.07 to +0.09 in each of June, July and August and on every device.\n\n### Caveats for the launch decision\n- v7_pd is trained on exploration logs, and those are the same logs I used to value it. If its training period overlaps this window, the +20% may be inflated by in-sample fit. The stable month-by-month lift is reassuring but doesn't rule this out. I'd ask Ranking for the training dates; if they overlap, re-run on later traffic or run a short A/B before a full rollout.\n- The method assumes a click on one slot depends on that title and its position, not on what else is in the row. The v6 and AB-1182 checks above support this.\n\n### Changed files\n`recs_eval/logs.py`, `replay.py`, `metrics.py`, `cli.py` and `report.py`, plus a 1.5.0 entry in `RELEASES.md`. `data/logs.sqlite` and the reports are untouched. The launch rule now picks the qualifying candidate with the biggest lift over v6; before, it compared their values, which comes to the same thing."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":672,"cache_read_input_tokens":49436,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":672,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"230ed98a-7798-4532-801d-718b6246ddc1","uuid":"0311678c-717c-4776-9d4f-dbc09310de6f","timestamp":"2026-10-01T06:01:40.944Z","request_id":"req_011Cfb1SZy5YsfZNLr6Af5Ap"}
